Washington Fishing Regulations

Not legal advice. Compiled from official government summaries. Regulations change each year — always check the official source before relying.

General Rules (barbless hooks are the #1 cause of fines)

  • ⚠ Possession limit for game fish is 2 daily limits in any form.
  • ⚠ It is unlawful to fish for or retain green sturgeon; Pacific/western brook/river lamprey may not be taken or used as bait.
  • ⚠ Use of gaff hooks restricted; bow/spear not allowed for game fish unless authorized.
  • ⚠ Many special-rule rivers and lakes require barbless or single-point hooks - always check the Special Rules.
  • ⚠ Salmon: anglers may not continue fishing for salmon after the adult portion of the daily limit is retained.

Species Limits

SpeciesLimitSize / Season
Largemouth bass5/day (lakes); no limit in rivers/streams/beaver pondsOnly bass under 12 in, plus 1 over 17 in, in lakes
Smallmouth bass10/day (lakes); no limit in rivers/streams/beaver pondsOnly 1 over 14 in in lakes
Walleye8/day (lakes)Min 12 in; only 1 over 22 in
Channel catfish5/day (lakes)No minimum; no limit in rivers/streams
Burbot (ling)5/dayNo minimum
Trout (brown, cutthroat, rainbow, golden, tiger, grayling)Rivers: 2/day; Lakes: 5/day (combined 5 if fishing both)Rivers min 8 in; lakes no minimum
Kokanee10/dayNo minimum
Tiger muskellunge1/dayMin 50 in
Whitefish15/dayNo minimum
Steelhead2/dayMin 20 in; ALL wild steelhead must be released
Crappie, perch, sunfish, suckers, pikeminnow, rock bass, striped bass, non-channel catfishNo daily limitNo minimum
Common carpNo limit (spear/bow allowed)
ShadNo limit (eulachon closed unless emergency rule)
White sturgeon (Columbia, Bonneville-McNary)1/day, 2 annual statewideMin 38 in (below The Dalles) / 43 in (above); max 54 in fork length
Northern pikeNo limit (must be dead before removal from riparian area)
BullfrogsNo limit (angling, dip net, gig, bow)

Waterbody-Specific Rules

AKL POND
  • Smallmouth bass Year-round No min. size. Daily limit 15. Only 1 over 14" may be retained.
  • Largemouth bass Year-round No min. size. Daily limit 10. Max. size 12", except 1 over 17" may be retained.
  • Channel catfish Year-round No min. size. Daily limit 10.
  • Walleye Year-round No min. size. Daily limit 16. Only 1 over 22" may be retained.
  • Other game fish Year-round Statewide min. size/daily limit.

  • [from the mouth (Bayview-Edison Rd. Bridge) to the yellow marker at the wing wall approx. 1,000' upstream CRC (816)] All species Aug. 1-Sept. 30 Stationary gear restriction. Night closure. Angler's must only use fish eggs (roe) for bait and may only use one single-point hook; the hook may not measure more than 1/2" from point to shank (hook gap) and may not have any weight attached or molded directly to the hook such as twitching jigs, swim jigs, or lead headed casting jigs. Other than a swivel, anglers may not add any weight to their line. Anglers may only retain fish hooked inside the mouth.

Seasons

Most waters open during game fish seasons; salmon runs vary by marine area/river (North of Falcon). Check emergency rules.

Licence Fees

Washington fishing license required; salmon endorsement/Catch Record Cards for salmon, steelhead, sturgeon, halibut; two-pole endorsement for second rod.
